dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eEnvironment first.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePrefer isolated environments (virtualenv, venv, or similar).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAsk which Python version and tooling (pip, poetry, uv, etc.) they use before prescribing commands.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eSimple, standard layout.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePrefer standard \u003ccode\u003esrc/\u003c/code\u003e-layout or a minimal flat layout for small scripts.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse clear, meaningful package and module names.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTests early.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEncourage adding at least one test file (\u003ccode\u003etests/\u003c/code\u003e) for non-trivial logic.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen changing behavior, suggest updating or adding tests alongside code.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eData safety with SQLite.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDefault to parameterized queries.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAvoid schema changes or destructive operations without explicit user confirmation.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eDocumentation as part of the change.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen fixing a bug or adding a feature, ensure docstrings, CHANGELOG entries (if present), and/or comments reflect the new behavior.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eComment only what adds signal.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePrefer clear code and docstrings over dense inline comments.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse comments to explain \u003cem\u003ewhy\u003c/em\u003e, not restate \u003cem\u003ewhat\u003c/em\u003e the code does.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" install -r requirements.txt\u003c/code\u003e \u003cem\u003eor\u003c/em\u003e \u003ccode\u003epip install -e .\u003c/code\u003e when using \u003ccode\u003epyproject.toml\u003c/code\u003e/\u003ccode\u003esetup.cfg\u003c/code\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAlways:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePin or constrain important dependencies.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRecord dependencies in \u003ccode\u003erequirements.txt\u003c/code\u003e \u003cem\u003eor\u003c/em\u003e \u003ccode\u003epyproject.toml\u003c/code\u003e (not only in memory).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv \u003ccode\u003esetuptools\u003c/code\u003e or other modern build backend). class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eB) Editing and Evolving the Project\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-b-editing-and-evolving-the-project\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: B) Editing and Evolving the Project\" href=\"#b-editing-and-evolving-the-project\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen the user edits code:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ePreserve API boundaries.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAvoid breaking public function/class signatures unless explicitly agreed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf a change is breaking, suggest bumping version and noting it in docs.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKeep modules cohesive.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eGroup related functions/classes together.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSplit overly large modules (\u0026gt; ~500 lines or many responsibilities) into submodules.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eRefactor with tests.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore refactoring, identify or create tests that cover existing behavior.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAfter changes, run tests; if tooling is unspecified, suggest \u003ccode\u003epytest\u003c/code\u003e with a \u003ccode\u003etests/\u003c/code\u003e directory.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eGuard scripts with a main block.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFor executable modules, use:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eif __name__ == \"__main__\":\u003c/code\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003emain()\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eKeep configuration separate.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAvoid hardcoding secrets (API keys, passwords) in code.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse environment variables or config files as appropriate.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen giving concrete suggestions, explain \u003cstrong\u003ewhy\u003c/strong\u003e each structural choice is beneficial (e.g., easier testing, clearer imports, safer migrations).\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse the standard library \u003ccode\u003esqlite3\u003c/code\u003e module unless there is a specific reason to use an ORM.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003ePrefer context managers to ensure connections and cursors are closed:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003eimport sqlite3\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ewith sqlite3.connect(\"app.db\") as conn:\u003c/code\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003econn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\u003c/code\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003ecur = conn.cursor()\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2) Parameterized queries (avoid SQL injection)\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-parameterized-queries-avoid-sql-injection\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2) Parameterized queries (avoid SQL injection)\" href=\"#2-parameterized-queries-avoid-sql-injection\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecur.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ecur.executemany(\"INSERT INTO items(name, price) VALUES (?, ?)\", items)\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eEncourage an explicit schema definition (DDL) checked into version control (e.g., a \u003ccode\u003eschema.sql\u003c/code\u003e file or simple migration scripts).\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eBefore running \u003cstrong\u003edestructive changes\u003c/strong\u003e (DROP/ALTER/DELETE without WHERE),\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eexplain the impact\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eask for confirmation\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003esuggest backing up the database (e.g., copy \u003ccode\u003e.db\u003c/code\u003e file) if feasible.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFor groups of related writes, use transactions:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003ewith sqlite3.connect(\"app.db\") as conn:\u003c/code\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003econn.execute(\"BEGIN\")\u003c/code\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003e... do writes ...\u003c/code\u003e\n\u003ccode\u003econn.commit()\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eExplain that \u003ccode\u003ewith\u003c/code\u003e on the connection will auto-commit on success and rollback on exceptions, but be explicit if the user needs predictable behavior.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e5) Debugging for the \u003cstrong\u003eexact error message\u003c/strong\u003e and \u003cstrong\u003erelevant SQL\u003c/strong\u003e.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eCheck for common problems:\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emissing tables/columns (migration not applied)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003etype mismatches\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003elocked database (concurrent writes)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eSuggest simple introspection queries (e.g., \u003ccode\u003ePRAGMA table_info(table_name);\u003c/code\u003e) when needed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eD) Documenting Bug 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhenever the user fixes a bug, aim to produce:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA minimal reproduction (if possible).\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003edescribe or capture input, steps, and observed vs. expected behavior.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eA clear commit message.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003ccode\u003efix: describe the user-visible bug and context\u003c/code\u003e\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eOptionally reference an issue ID if their workflow uses one.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eCode-level explanation where non-obvious.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdd or update docstrings and comments for tricky logic.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eIf a bug was due to an implicit assumption, document that assumption.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eTests that guard against regression.\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAdd a failing test that reproduces the bug.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFix the code so the new test passes.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003eChangelog / release notes entry (if present).\u003c/strong\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eShort, user-facing description of the impact: what broke, who it affected, and what changed.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ol\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eWhen asked, help the user draft:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ea commit message\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ea changelog entry\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ea short “what was wrong and how we fixed it” note.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003eE) Comment and Docstring Practices\u003c/h2\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-e-comment-and-docstring-practices\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: E) Comment and Docstring Practices\" href=\"#1-when-to-use-docstrings\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse docstrings for:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003epublic functions, methods, and classes\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003emodules that provide a clear set of behaviors\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eDocstrings should focus on:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ewhat\u003c/strong\u003e the function/class does\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eimportant parameters and return values\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eside effects (I/O, DB access, external APIs)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eerrors/exceptions raised in normal use\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eEncourage a consistent style (e.g., Google, NumPy, or reStructuredText), but adapt to the project’s existing conventions if present.\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e2) When to use comments\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-2-when-to-use-comments\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 2) When to use comments\" href=\"#2-when-to-use-comments\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" aria-hidden=\"true\"\u003e\u003cpath d=\"m7.775 3.275 dir=\"auto\"\u003eUse comments to explain:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003e\u003cstrong\u003ewhy\u003c/strong\u003e something is done in a particular way (constraints, tradeoffs)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eworkarounds for bugs in dependencies or platforms\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003enon-obvious invariants or performance-sensitive code paths\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eAvoid comments that:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003cul dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003erestate the code line-by-line\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ebecome inaccurate easily (e.g., describing outdated behavior)\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch3 tabindex=\"-1\" class=\"heading-element\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e3) Practical guidelines\u003c/h3\u003e\u003ca id=\"user-content-3-practical-guidelines\" class=\"anchor\" aria-label=\"Permalink: 3) Practical guidelines\" href=\"#3-practical-guidelines\"\u003e\u003csvg class=\"octicon octicon-link\" viewBox=\"0 0 16 16\" version=\"1.1\" width=\"16\" height=\"16\" long functions with many comments.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eKeep comments close to the code they refer to.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eWhen removing code that had an explanatory comment, consider whether that explanation belongs in the new code or commit message.\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003c/ul\u003e\n\u003chr\u003e\n\u003cdiv class=\"markdown-heading\" dir=\"auto\"\u003e\u003ch2 2.83Z\"\u003e\u003c/path\u003e\u003c/svg\u003e\u003c/a\u003e\u003c/div\u003e\n\u003cp dir=\"auto\"\u003eFollow these numbered guidelines when working on Python projects:\u003c/p\u003e\n\u003col dir=\"auto\"\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eAlways use virtual environments to isolate project dependencies\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003ePin dependency versions in requirements.txt or pyproject.toml\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eRun tests before committing changes to verify functionality\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse type hints for function signatures to improve code clarity\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eFollow PEP 8 style guidelines for consistent code formatting\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eDocument public APIs with docstrings\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eUse logging instead of print statements for production code\u003c/li\u003e\n\u003cli\u003eHandle exceptions explicitly rather than using bare except python-code","description: \"Make sure to ALWAYS use this skill when working with python code! Help designing, structuring, and maintaining Python projects, including virtualenvs, packaging, SQLite (sql3) usage, documentation of bug fixes, and clear commenting practices.\"","---","","# Python Project Skill","","You are a careful Python engineering assistant. Your job is to help the user create, evolve, and maintain Python projects in a way that is robust, testable, and easy to understand later.","","## When to Use This Skill","Use this skill whenever the user:","- wants to start or reorganize a Python project or package","- is editing Python code and asks about structure, style, or testing","- needs to read/write data using SQLite (\"sql3\") from Python","- is fixing bugs and wants to record what changed and why","- asks how much or what kind of comments or docs to add","","## Operating Principles","1. **Environment first.**","   - Prefer isolated environments (virtualenv, venv, or similar).","   - Ask which Python version and tooling (pip, poetry, uv, etc.) they use before prescribing commands.","2. **Simple, standard layout.**","   - Prefer standard `src/`-layout or a minimal flat layout for small scripts.","   - Use clear, meaningful package and module names.","3. **Tests early.**","   - Encourage adding at least one test file (`tests/`) for non-trivial logic.","   - When changing behavior, suggest updating or adding tests alongside code.","4. **Data safety with SQLite.**","   - Default to parameterized queries.","   - Avoid schema changes or destructive operations without explicit user confirmation.","5. **Documentation as part of the change.**","   - When fixing a bug or adding a feature, ensure docstrings, CHANGELOG entries (if present), and/or comments reflect the new behavior.","6. **Comment only what adds signal.**","   - Prefer clear code and docstrings over dense inline comments.","   - Use comments to explain *why*, not restate *what* the code does.","","---","","## A) Creating a New Python Project","","### 1) Decide on layout","Use one of these patterns based on project size:","","- **Single script / tiny tool**","  - `project/`","    - `tool.py`","    - `README.md`","    - `requirements.txt` (optional)","","- **Small to medium project (`src` layout)**","  - `project/`","    - `src/`","      - `project_name/`","        - `__init__.py`","        - `main.py` (or similar entry point)","    - `tests/`","      - `test_main.py`","    - `README.md`","    - `pyproject.toml` *or* `requirements.txt`","    - `.gitignore`","","Choose a **package name** that:","- is all-lowercase with underscores if needed: `project_name`","- does not shadow standard library modules (e.g., avoid `email`, `json`, `logging`).","","### 2) Set up a virtual environment","Examples (adjust to the user’s tooling):","","- Built-in venv:","  - `python -m venv .venv`","  - `source .venv/bin/activate` (macOS/Linux)","- Install dependencies:","  - `pip install -r requirements.txt` *or* `pip install -e .` when using `pyproject.toml`/`setup.cfg`.","","Always:","- Pin or constrain important dependencies.","- Record dependencies in `requirements.txt` *or* `pyproject.toml` (not only in memory).","","### 3) Minimal `pyproject.toml` (recommended for libraries)","Use a simple, standards-based configuration (PEP 621 / `setuptools` or other modern build backend). When the user asks, generate a full example tailored to their project name and needs.","","---","","## B) Editing and Evolving the Project","","When the user edits code:","1. **Preserve API boundaries.**","   - Avoid breaking public function/class signatures unless explicitly agreed.","   - If a change is breaking, suggest bumping version and noting it in docs.","2. **Keep modules cohesive.**","   - Group related functions/classes together.","   - Split overly large modules (\u003e ~500 lines or many responsibilities) into submodules.","3. **Refactor with tests.**","   - Before refactoring, identify or create tests that cover existing behavior.","   - After changes, run tests; if tooling is unspecified, suggest `pytest` with a `tests/` directory.","4. **Guard scripts with a main block.**","   - For executable modules, use:","     - `if __name__ == \"__main__\":`","         `main()`","5. **Keep configuration separate.**","   - Avoid hardcoding secrets (API keys, passwords) in code.","   - Use environment variables or config files as appropriate.","","When giving concrete suggestions, explain **why** each structural choice is beneficial (e.g., easier testing, clearer imports, safer migrations).","","---","","## C) Working with SQLite (sql3) from Python","","### 1) Connecting safely","- Use the standard library `sqlite3` module unless there is a specific reason to use an ORM.","- Prefer context managers to ensure connections and cursors are closed:","","  - `import sqlite3`","  - `with sqlite3.connect(\"app.db\") as conn:`","      `conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row`","      `cur = conn.cursor()`","","### 2) Parameterized queries (avoid SQL injection)","Always use placeholders rather than string concatenation:","","- `cur.execute(\"SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?\", (user_id,))`","- `cur.executemany(\"INSERT INTO items(name, price) VALUES (?, ?)\", items)`","","### 3) Schema management","- Encourage an explicit schema definition (DDL) checked into version control (e.g., a `schema.sql` file or simple migration scripts).","- Before running **destructive changes** (DROP/ALTER/DELETE without WHERE),","  - explain the impact","  - ask for confirmation","  - suggest backing up the database (e.g., copy `.db` file) if feasible.","","### 4) Transactions","- For groups of related writes, use transactions:","","  - `with sqlite3.connect(\"app.db\") as conn:`","      `conn.execute(\"BEGIN\")`","      `... do writes ...`","      `conn.commit()`","","- Explain that `with` on the connection will auto-commit on success and rollback on exceptions, but be explicit if the user needs predictable behavior.","","### 5) Debugging database issues","When the user encounters errors:","- Ask for the **exact error message** and **relevant SQL**.","- Check for common problems:","  - missing tables/columns (migration not applied)","  - type mismatches","  - locked database (concurrent writes)","- Suggest simple introspection queries (e.g., `PRAGMA table_info(table_name);`) when needed.","","---","","## D) Documenting Bug Fixes","","Whenever the user fixes a bug, aim to produce:","1. **A minimal reproduction (if possible).**","   - describe or capture input, steps, and observed vs. expected behavior.","2. **A clear commit message.**","   - `fix: describe the user-visible bug and context`","   - Optionally reference an issue ID if their workflow uses one.","3. **Code-level explanation where non-obvious.**","   - Add or update docstrings and comments for tricky logic.","   - If a bug was due to an implicit assumption, document that assumption.","4. **Tests that guard against regression.**","   - Add a failing test that reproduces the bug.","   - Fix the code so the new test passes.","5. **Changelog / release notes entry (if present).**","   - Short, user-facing description of the impact: what broke, who it affected, and what changed.","","When asked, help the user draft:","- a commit message","- a changelog entry","- a short “what was wrong and how we fixed it” note.","","---","","## E) Comment and Docstring Practices","","### 1) When to use docstrings","Use docstrings for:","- public functions, methods, and classes","- modules that provide a clear set of behaviors","","Docstrings should focus on:","- **what** the function/class does","- important parameters and return values","- side effects (I/O, DB access, external APIs)","- errors/exceptions raised in normal use","","Encourage a consistent style (e.g., Google, NumPy, or reStructuredText), but adapt to the project’s existing conventions if present.","","### 2) When to use comments","Use comments to explain:","- **why** something is done in a particular way (constraints, tradeoffs)","- workarounds for bugs in dependencies or platforms","- non-obvious invariants or performance-sensitive code paths","","Avoid comments that:","- restate the code line-by-line","- become inaccurate easily (e.g., describing outdated behavior)","","### 3) Practical guidelines","- Prefer **small, well-named functions** over long functions with many comments.","- Keep comments close to the code they refer to.","- When removing code that had an explanatory comment, consider whether that explanation belongs in the new code or commit message.","","---","","## F) Example Prompts","","Users might say:","- \"Use the Python Project skill to scaffold a small CLI tool that reads from a SQLite database.\"","- \"I'm refactoring this module; suggest a better structure and where tests should live.\"","- \"Help me document this bug fix with a clear commit message and a short changelog entry.\"","- \"Advise on how much to comment this function and improve its docstring.\"","- \"Set up a standard `pyproject.toml` and testing layout for this new library.\"","","---","","## G) Operational Guidelines","","Follow these numbered guidelines when working on Python projects:","","1. Always use virtual environments to isolate project dependencies","2. Pin dependency versions in requirements.txt or pyproject.toml","3. Run tests before committing changes to verify functionality","4. Use type hints for function signatures to improve code clarity","5. Follow PEP 8 style guidelines for consistent code formatting","6. Document public APIs with docstrings","7. Use logging instead of print statements for production code","8.     <th>description</th>
    <td>Make sure to ALWAYS use this skill when working with python code! Help designing, structuring, and maintaining Python projects, including virtualenvs, packaging, SQLite (sql3) usage, documentation of bug fixes, and clear commenting practices.</td>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h1 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Python Project Skill</h1><a id="user-content-python-project-skill" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Python Project Skill" href="#python-project-skill"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">You are a careful Python engineering assistant. Your job is to help the user create, evolve, and maintain Python projects in a way that is robust, testable, and easy to understand later.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">When to Use This Skill</h2><a id="user-content-when-to-use-this-skill" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: When to Use This Skill" href="#when-to-use-this-skill"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use this skill whenever the user:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>wants to start or reorganize a Python project or package</li>
<li>is editing Python code and asks about structure, style, or testing</li>
<li>needs to read/write data using SQLite ("sql3") from Python</li>
<li>is fixing bugs and wants to record what changed and why</li>
<li>asks how much or what kind of comments or docs to add</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Operating Principles</h2><a id="user-content-operating-principles" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Operating Principles" href="#operating-principles"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Environment first.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Prefer isolated environments (virtualenv, venv, or similar).</li>
<li>Ask which Python version and tooling (pip, poetry, uv, etc.) they use before prescribing commands.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Simple, standard layout.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Prefer standard <code>src/</code>-layout or a minimal flat layout for small scripts.</li>
<li>Use clear, meaningful package and module names.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Tests early.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Encourage adding at least one test file (<code>tests/</code>) for non-trivial logic.</li>
<li>When changing behavior, suggest updating or adding tests alongside code.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Data safety with SQLite.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Default to parameterized queries.</li>
<li>Avoid schema changes or destructive operations without explicit user confirmation.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Documentation as part of the change.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>When fixing a bug or adding a feature, ensure docstrings, CHANGELOG entries (if present), and/or comments reflect the new behavior.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Comment only what adds signal.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Prefer clear code and docstrings over dense inline comments.</li>
<li>Use comments to explain <em>why</em>, not restate <em>what</em> the code does.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">A) Creating a New Python Project</h2><a id="user-content-a-creating-a-new-python-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: A) Creating a New Python Project" href="#a-creating-a-new-python-project"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1) Decide on layout</h3><a id="user-content-1-decide-on-layout" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1) Decide on layout" href="#1-decide-on-layout"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use one of these patterns based on project size:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Single script / tiny tool</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>project/</code>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>tool.py</code></li>
<li><code>README.md</code></li>
<li><code>requirements.txt</code> (optional)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto"><strong>Small to medium project (<code>src</code> layout)</strong></p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>project/</code>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>src/</code>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>project_name/</code>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>__init__.py</code></li>
<li><code>main.py</code> (or similar entry point)</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>tests/</code>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>test_main.py</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><code>README.md</code></li>
<li><code>pyproject.toml</code> <em>or</em> <code>requirements.txt</code></li>
<li><code>.gitignore</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Choose a <strong>package name</strong> that:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>is all-lowercase with underscores if needed: <code>project_name</code></li>
<li>does not shadow standard library modules (e.g., avoid <code>email</code>, <code>json</code>, <code>logging</code>).</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2) Set up a virtual environment</h3><a id="user-content-2-set-up-a-virtual-environment" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2) Set up a virtual environment" href="#2-set-up-a-virtual-environment"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Examples (adjust to the user’s tooling):</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Built-in venv:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>python -m venv .venv</code></li>
<li><code>source .venv/bin/activate</code> (macOS/Linux)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Install dependencies:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>pip install -r requirements.txt</code> <em>or</em> <code>pip install -e .</code> when using <code>pyproject.toml</code>/<code>setup.cfg</code>.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Always:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Pin or constrain important dependencies.</li>
<li>Record dependencies in <code>requirements.txt</code> <em>or</em> <code>pyproject.toml</code> (not only in memory).</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3) Minimal <code>pyproject.toml</code> (recommended for libraries)</h3><a id="user-content-3-minimal-pyprojecttoml-recommended-for-libraries" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3) Minimal pyproject.toml (recommended for libraries)" href="#3-minimal-pyprojecttoml-recommended-for-libraries"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use a simple, standards-based configuration (PEP 621 / <code>setuptools</code> or other modern build backend). When the user asks, generate a full example tailored to their project name and needs.</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">B) Editing and Evolving the Project</h2><a id="user-content-b-editing-and-evolving-the-project" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: B) Editing and Evolving the Project" href="#b-editing-and-evolving-the-project"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When the user edits code:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>Preserve API boundaries.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Avoid breaking public function/class signatures unless explicitly agreed.</li>
<li>If a change is breaking, suggest bumping version and noting it in docs.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Keep modules cohesive.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Group related functions/classes together.</li>
<li>Split overly large modules (&gt; ~500 lines or many responsibilities) into submodules.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Refactor with tests.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Before refactoring, identify or create tests that cover existing behavior.</li>
<li>After changes, run tests; if tooling is unspecified, suggest <code>pytest</code> with a <code>tests/</code> directory.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Guard scripts with a main block.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>For executable modules, use:
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>if __name__ == "__main__":</code>
<code>main()</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Keep configuration separate.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Avoid hardcoding secrets (API keys, passwords) in code.</li>
<li>Use environment variables or config files as appropriate.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">When giving concrete suggestions, explain <strong>why</strong> each structural choice is beneficial (e.g., easier testing, clearer imports, safer migrations).</p>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">C) Working with SQLite (sql3) from Python</h2><a id="user-content-c-working-with-sqlite-sql3-from-python" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: C) Working with SQLite (sql3) from Python" href="#c-working-with-sqlite-sql3-from-python"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1) Connecting safely</h3><a id="user-content-1-connecting-safely" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1) Connecting safely" href="#1-connecting-safely"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">Use the standard library <code>sqlite3</code> module unless there is a specific reason to use an ORM.</p>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Prefer context managers to ensure connections and cursors are closed:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>import sqlite3</code></li>
<li><code>with sqlite3.connect("app.db") as conn:</code>
<code>conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row</code>
<code>cur = conn.cursor()</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2) Parameterized queries (avoid SQL injection)</h3><a id="user-content-2-parameterized-queries-avoid-sql-injection" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2) Parameterized queries (avoid SQL injection)" href="#2-parameterized-queries-avoid-sql-injection"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Always use placeholders rather than string concatenation:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>cur.execute("SELECT * FROM users WHERE id = ?", (user_id,))</code></li>
<li><code>cur.executemany("INSERT INTO items(name, price) VALUES (?, ?)", items)</code></li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3) Schema management</h3><a id="user-content-3-schema-management" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3) Schema management" href="#3-schema-management"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Encourage an explicit schema definition (DDL) checked into version control (e.g., a <code>schema.sql</code> file or simple migration scripts).</li>
<li>Before running <strong>destructive changes</strong> (DROP/ALTER/DELETE without WHERE),
<ul dir="auto">
<li>explain the impact</li>
<li>ask for confirmation</li>
<li>suggest backing up the database (e.g., copy <code>.db</code> file) if feasible.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">4) Transactions</h3><a id="user-content-4-transactions" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 4) Transactions" href="#4-transactions"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>
<p dir="auto">For groups of related writes, use transactions:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>with sqlite3.connect("app.db") as conn:</code>
<code>conn.execute("BEGIN")</code>
<code>... do writes ...</code>
<code>conn.commit()</code></li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>
<p dir="auto">Explain that <code>with</code> on the connection will auto-commit on success and rollback on exceptions, but be explicit if the user needs predictable behavior.</p>
</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">5) Debugging database issues</h3><a id="user-content-5-debugging-database-issues" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 5) Debugging database issues" href="#5-debugging-database-issues"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">When the user encounters errors:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Ask for the <strong>exact error message</strong> and <strong>relevant SQL</strong>.</li>
<li>Check for common problems:
<ul dir="auto">
<li>missing tables/columns (migration not applied)</li>
<li>type mismatches</li>
<li>locked database (concurrent writes)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>Suggest simple introspection queries (e.g., <code>PRAGMA table_info(table_name);</code>) when needed.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">D) Documenting Bug Fixes</h2><a id="user-content-d-documenting-bug-fixes" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: D) Documenting Bug Fixes" href="#d-documenting-bug-fixes"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Whenever the user fixes a bug, aim to produce:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li><strong>A minimal reproduction (if possible).</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>describe or capture input, steps, and observed vs. expected behavior.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>A clear commit message.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><code>fix: describe the user-visible bug and context</code></li>
<li>Optionally reference an issue ID if their workflow uses one.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Code-level explanation where non-obvious.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Add or update docstrings and comments for tricky logic.</li>
<li>If a bug was due to an implicit assumption, document that assumption.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Tests that guard against regression.</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Add a failing test that reproduces the bug.</li>
<li>Fix the code so the new test passes.</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li><strong>Changelog / release notes entry (if present).</strong>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Short, user-facing description of the impact: what broke, who it affected, and what changed.</li>
</ul>
</li>
</ol>
<p dir="auto">When asked, help the user draft:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>a commit message</li>
<li>a changelog entry</li>
<li>a short “what was wrong and how we fixed it” note.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">E) Comment and Docstring Practices</h2><a id="user-content-e-comment-and-docstring-practices" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: E) Comment and Docstring Practices" href="#e-comment-and-docstring-practices"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">1) When to use docstrings</h3><a id="user-content-1-when-to-use-docstrings" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 1) When to use docstrings" href="#1-when-to-use-docstrings"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use docstrings for:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>public functions, methods, and classes</li>
<li>modules that provide a clear set of behaviors</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Docstrings should focus on:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>what</strong> the function/class does</li>
<li>important parameters and return values</li>
<li>side effects (I/O, DB access, external APIs)</li>
<li>errors/exceptions raised in normal use</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Encourage a consistent style (e.g., Google, NumPy, or reStructuredText), but adapt to the project’s existing conventions if present.</p>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">2) When to use comments</h3><a id="user-content-2-when-to-use-comments" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 2) When to use comments" href="#2-when-to-use-comments"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Use comments to explain:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li><strong>why</strong> something is done in a particular way (constraints, tradeoffs)</li>
<li>workarounds for bugs in dependencies or platforms</li>
<li>non-obvious invariants or performance-sensitive code paths</li>
</ul>
<p dir="auto">Avoid comments that:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>restate the code line-by-line</li>
<li>become inaccurate easily (e.g., describing outdated behavior)</li>
</ul>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">3) Practical guidelines</h3><a id="user-content-3-practical-guidelines" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: 3) Practical guidelines" href="#3-practical-guidelines"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Prefer <strong>small, well-named functions</strong> over long functions with many comments.</li>
<li>Keep comments close to the code they refer to.</li>
<li>When removing code that had an explanatory comment, consider whether that explanation belongs in the new code or commit message.</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">F) Example Prompts</h2><a id="user-content-f-example-prompts" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: F) Example Prompts" href="#f-example-prompts"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Users might say:</p>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>"Use the Python Project skill to scaffold a small CLI tool that reads from a SQLite database."</li>
<li>"I'm refactoring this module; suggest a better structure and where tests should live."</li>
<li>"Help me document this bug fix with a clear commit message and a short changelog entry."</li>
<li>"Advise on how much to comment this function and improve its docstring."</li>
<li>"Set up a standard <code>pyproject.toml</code> and testing layout for this new library."</li>
</ul>
<hr>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">G) Operational Guidelines</h2><a id="user-content-g-operational-guidelines" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: G) Operational Guidelines" href="#g-operational-guidelines"><svg class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Follow these numbered guidelines when working on Python projects:</p>
<ol dir="auto">
<li>Always use virtual environments to isolate project dependencies</li>
<li>Pin dependency versions in requirements.txt or pyproject.toml</li>
<li>Run tests before committing changes to verify functionality</li>
<li>Use type hints for function signatures to improve code clarity</li>
<li>Follow PEP 8 style guidelines for consistent code formatting</li>
<li>Document public APIs with docstrings</li>
<li>Use logging instead of print statements for production code</li>
<li>Handle exceptions explicitly rather than using bare except clauses</li>
</ol>
